Does not edit or write files. ([AneliiaOleksiuk/dev-digest-ai-marketplace](https://github.com/AneliiaOleksiuk/dev-digest-ai-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/researcher/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Read-only web research agent for Aqmen projects, designed to run in PARALLEL with sibling researchers on disjoint assignments. Two missions chosen by the spawning prompt — "scoping-scan" (before a model exists: top-down estimates, segmentation conventions, the driver data landscape, players and their disclosures) and "value-package" (after the grain is set: real, cited figures for an assigned set of drivers, companies or fields). Works for market sizing, company analysis, competitive landscapes and custom models. Returns LOAD-READY packets — tables with units and scale, the documents to store… ([aqmen-ai/aqmen-skills](https://github.com/aqmen-ai/aqmen-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/aqmen/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: 기술 리서치 전문가. 라이브러리 문서 조회, API 사용법 확인, 최신 기술 동향 조사. 기술적 질문이나 구현 방법 조사 시 사용. ([aquisto535/mind-palette-project](https://github.com/aquisto535/mind-palette-project/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Seahorse RESEARCHER — Opus-tier discovery and literature agent. Fan-out web + primary-source research (arXiv via alphaXiv, ResearchGate, web scraping), adversarial verification, cited synthesis. Invoke for any research, discovery, or "find out X from real sources" task. ([Archit3115/seahorse](https://github.com/Archit3115/seahorse/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Deep codebase researcher. Use when exploring how a system works, mapping data flows, or gathering context before building a feature. ([arndvs/ctrlshft](https://github.com/arndvs/ctrlshft/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Gathers version-accurate documentation and produces a ResearchPack ([AskMP/St-ged](https://github.com/AskMP/St-ged/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Use proactively when a question requires reading multiple files, scanning a codebase, or exploring unfamiliar code. The researcher reads broadly and returns a compressed summary instead of polluting the main context with raw file dumps. Ideal first step before any non-trivial change. ([atalias-mkyle/claude-dev-kit](https://github.com/atalias-mkyle/claude-dev-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Recherche technique Flare/Chainlink/Arc ([Ayoub-ouederni/SENTINEL](https://github.com/Ayoub-ouederni/SENTINEL/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: I'm your dedicated Researcher, specializing in deep-dive investigations, comprehensive analysis, and knowledge synthesis. My core responsibilities include: ([bailejl/Product-Outcomes](https://github.com/bailejl/Product-Outcomes/blob/HEAD/.roo/hive-mind/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Fast external/web research. Use for the research workflow's web legs — investigate a topic, product, API, or library on the open web and report back against a shared reporting frame with concrete sources. Read-only; never edits. Runs on Haiku. ([bakerwebsolutions/auxilia](https://github.com/bakerwebsolutions/auxilia/blob/HEAD/plugins/orchestrate/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Web検索・公開情報の収集を大量に行い、出典付きの構造化された調査結果を返す専任エージェント。企業調査の情報収集フェーズや、会計基準の原文・改正状況の確認など、「検索回数が多いが高度な判断を要しない」収集作業に使用する。会計上の判断や不正リスクの評価は行わない(それはメインセッションの仕事)。 &lt;example&gt; Context: 企業調査タスクで、対象会社の基礎情報を集める段階 user: "株式会社〇〇の企業調査をして" assistant: "まず researcher エージェントに基礎情報(概要・事業・業績・株主・ニュース)の収集を委譲します。" &lt;commentary&gt; 情報収集はトークン消費が多いが推論難度が低いため、低コストモデルで実行してコストを抑える。 &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([barorin/cpa-toolkit](https://github.com/barorin/cpa-toolkit/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Works only within a story a person started with /sdlc:next, when that runbook delegates this gate; not for general review, security, performance, testing, planning, analysis or retro requests. Gate 2 analysis agent. Use it to analyse a selected story against the specification, the codebase map and the architecture contract, and to produce the analysis, the threat assessment and a security-sensitivity decision before any tests or plan are written. Fresh context; stores its documents through `sdlc artifact write` and otherwise writes only under .sdlc/stories/&lt;ID&gt;/ and CODEMAP.md. ([bbsnly/sdlc](https://github.com/bbsnly/sdlc/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Research agent for gathering and summarizing information from the web, documentation, and external APIs. Use this agent when you need to investigate libraries, compare approaches, look up API docs, or summarize technical concepts before implementation. Returns concise findings — does not write code. ([benaus187/TRAIVEL](https://github.com/benaus187/TRAIVEL/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Use this agent to explore and understand the KVWN codebase. Finds files, traces call paths, identifies reusable utilities, reads Supabase migrations, and answers questions about how things currently work. Read-only — never writes code. Use before planning or implementing any non-trivial change. ([Benjamin-Seiberl/KVWN](https://github.com/Benjamin-Seiberl/KVWN/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Resolve current technical questions through primary sources and repository evidence. Compare options, track coverage and uncertainty, and return actionable findings without editing files. ([BharathChinthallapalli/reusable-github-template](https://github.com/BharathChinthallapalli/reusable-github-template/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/researcher.agent.md))
- **researcher**: Web and documentation research that needs synthesis — comparing sources, benchmarks, prices, APIs, docs and release notes — returning a compact, sourced report. Use this instead of an ad-hoc general-purpose agent for research: this agent's effort is pinned, while general-purpose inherits the session's higher effort. Not for codebase search (locate) or summarising one known file (digest). ([Bijaykars/claude-code-autoroute](https://github.com/Bijaykars/claude-code-autoroute/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Read-only research and codebase exploration, kept on a cheap tier. Delegate here to map how something works, find where a symbol is defined or used, gather the facts an edit needs (importers/callers, blast radius, real data schemas), compare approaches, or answer an open question that spans many files (any task that reads and reports but never writes). Keeps the main session's context clean and runs on a cheap tier regardless of the main session's model. Prefer this over exploring inline whenever the answer requires reading more than a couple of files. ([blineadam/agent-plan-and-track](https://github.com/blineadam/agent-plan-and-track/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: 当自动修复失败时进行深度研究。接收包含代码上下文的错误报告，返回解决方案或承认失败。 ([blueif16/amazing-claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/blueif16/amazing-claude-code-plugins/blob/HEAD/infistack/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Research specialist for comparing technologies, evaluating libraries, and reading official documentation. Use for research tasks involving technical comparison, architecture decisions, or library evaluation. ([blueif16/amazing-claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/blueif16/amazing-claude-code-plugins/blob/HEAD/GoDag/agents/.researcher.md))
- **researcher**: The Researcher agent. Invoke when HR needs to know which existing agents can handle a set of task phases, or when a new agent spec needs to be drafted. Researcher audits the codebase and the Agent Registry, scores existing agents against the current task, and either confirms a fit or produces a detailed spec for a new agent. Always invoke Researcher before HR makes any hiring or commissioning decision. ([blur94/skills](https://github.com/blur94/skills/blob/HEAD/skills/misc/ceo-pipeline/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: You are a research agent. You can explore the codebase and search the web, but you cannot modify any files. ([bottah/agent-bobs](https://github.com/bottah/agent-bobs/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Use before a decision that depends on material outside this conversation - product documentation, API references, competitor pages, standards, long PDFs, release notes. Returns a short summary with sources. Do NOT use for material already in the conversation, and do NOT use it to make the decision itself. ([brandonhall/claude-model-routing](https://github.com/brandonhall/claude-model-routing/blob/HEAD/plugins/model-routing/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: 조사 전담 에이전트. 코드베이스 탐색, 버그 근본 원인 분석, 기술 선택지 비교를 수행한다. 산출물 생성이 아닌 "이해"가 목적이다. oh-my-gx:gx-tdd 구현 단계의 정체 에스컬레이션뿐 아니라 독립적으로도 호출 가능하다. &lt;example&gt; User: 로그인 실패가 간헐적으로 발생하는 원인 조사 요청 Agent: 가설 3개 수립 → git blame/log로 최근 변경 추적 → 세션 타임아웃 설정 변경이 원인으로 확인 → 조사 보고서 출력 &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; User: 결제 모듈의 구조 파악 요청 Agent: 진입점에서 시작하여 호출 체인 추적 → 레이어 구조, 핵심 클래스, 외부 연동 지점 정리 → 탐색 보고서 출력 &lt;/example&gt; ([bs-koo/oh-my-gx](https://github.com/bs-koo/oh-my-gx/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Read-only pre-implementation researcher for dependency evaluation, codebase pattern analysis, prior-art review, and technical context gathering. ([buenhyden/video-analysis](https://github.com/buenhyden/video-analysis/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Research and documentation lookup - library docs, API patterns, guidance ([c-daly/agent-swarm](https://github.com/c-daly/agent-swarm/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Deep-dive research orchestrator that grows the project's research library. Use when the user gives a topic to investigate in depth. Researches directly, fans out to child researchers only for independent subquestions, and writes a cited wiki entry. - **researcher**: External evidence-gathering specialist — searches the open internet and academic literature for primary sources, evaluates their quality, and documents findings with verifiable URLs. Never fabricates sources — every claim must have a URL. Trigger phrases: "find sources", "search for evidence", "look this up", "verify this claim", "find papers on", "what does the literature say", "gather evidence", "r… ([carelvniekerk/agentic-skills](https://github.com/carelvniekerk/agentic-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/research/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Use PROACTIVELY when you need to understand how something works before implementing. Explores codebase without polluting main context. ([CarmiBanxe/banxe-emi-stack](https://github.com/CarmiBanxe/banxe-emi-stack/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: 외부 라이브러리·SDK·API 공식 문서를 조사해 결론과 근거만 압축해 돌려준다. Spring Boot·소셜 로그인 provider·AWS 등 우리 코드 밖 지식이 필요할 때, 특히 2개 이상 문서를 비교해야 할 때 사용. ([Central-MakeUs/TripFit-server](https://github.com/Central-MakeUs/TripFit-server/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: 웹 리서치 워커 서브에이전트. 할당된 소주제에 대해 WebSearch→WebFetch로 신뢰 소스를 모아 출처 추적 가능한 구조화 findings(소주제 정리 + 주장↔소스 매핑 + 게시일·조회일 소스 목록)를 **텍스트로 반환**한다(파일을 쓰지 않음 → 메인 컨텍스트 보호). 보통 `research` 오케스트레이터 스킬이 소주제별로 병렬 디스패치한다. &lt;example&gt; Context: research 오케스트레이터가 "GraphQL vs REST 성능" 주제를 분해해 소주제를 위임 user: "소주제 'GraphQL over-fetching 실측 데이터'를 한국어로 조사해 구조화 findings로 반환해줘" assistant: "researcher 에이전트로 해당 소주제를 수집하겠습니다." &lt;commentary&gt; 오케스트레이터가 격리된 워커에 수집을 위임하는 전형적 상황 — researcher를 사용한다. &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; Context: 좁은 단일 주제라 오케스트레이터가 워커 1개만 디스패치 user: "'OpenAI structured outputs 공식 스펙'을 1차 자료 중심으로 조사해줘" assist… ([centurio1987/my-claude-plugins](https://github.com/centurio1987/my-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/web-research/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Explores a codebase to answer specific questions or build context for a new feature. Returns a structured summary. Use this when context-building would otherwise pollute the main session. ([Chawengwit/PPY](https://github.com/Chawengwit/PPY/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Invoke when company or JD research is needed. Gathers company information, JD requirements, tech stack, etc. via web search. ([chenjingdev/chenjing-plugins](https://github.com/chenjingdev/chenjing-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/resume/.claude/agents/researcher.md))

- **researcher**: Independent research subagent for SPADES. Performs landscape research, prior-art lookups, library/SOTA evaluation, and external documentation reads, returning a condensed structured findings document. Spawned by /spades:research; never invoke directly. ([ChrisFmlyc/spades](https://github.com/ChrisFmlyc/spades/blob/HEAD/plugins/spades/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Researches topics by selecting the appropriate method. Routes Python package research to pkgq MCP tool and other research to c3:research skill. Examples: "research best practices for X", "investigate Y library options", "find info on package Z". ([christophevg/c3](https://github.com/christophevg/c3/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Technical research on APIs, libraries, patterns, best practices. Use in parallel for investigating different topics before planning. ([chubedan4605/SMART_RESTAURANT_CBD](https://github.com/chubedan4605/SMART_RESTAURANT_CBD/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Investigates technical unknowns and writes findings to docs/RESEARCH/. Use when: external API questions, unfamiliar library usage, architecture trade-off analysis. ([Chulf58/FORGE](https://github.com/Chulf58/FORGE/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Knowledge-gap filler for any phase. Does not modify the repository — it gathers only. Note it is not sandboxed: it holds WebFetch/WebSearch egress and runs the Context7 CLI via npx, so it fetches remote content and executes an npm package. Returns a synthesized answer with sources. ([Concord-Voice/agentic-dev-tooling](https://github.com/Concord-Voice/agentic-dev-tooling/blob/HEAD/plugins/core-lifecycle/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: ContextHub-aware research agent that uses project context, missions, and knowledge base to investigate questions and gather information. ([contexthub-md/marketplace](https://github.com/contexthub-md/marketplace/blob/HEAD/contexthub/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Gathers and curates knowledge from NotebookLM notebooks following research-lab:gather, and answers focused facet queries against a curated notebook. Spawnable N times for parallel coverage via Workflow agentType. ([cosmicdreams/claude-plugins](https://github.com/cosmicdreams/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/research-lab/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Phase 1에서 문서 하나당 하나씩 띄운다. 펜스 안이 그대로 넘어가는 프롬프트 본문이고, 대괄호 슬롯만 채운다. 아래 세 줄은 넘기지 않는다. ([Cotidie/cotidie-skills](https://github.com/Cotidie/cotidie-skills/blob/HEAD/claude/skills/explain-concept/agents/researcher.md) · 2 copies)
- **researcher**: Gathers and distills information for planning — from the web, GitHub, local projects, files, and databases — into cited research notes under .docs/research/. Typical triggers include the orchestrator needing context before an ADR or spec, the owner asking to investigate a topic, or a planner needing prior art. See "When to invoke" in the body. ([craigeous/loom](https://github.com/craigeous/loom/blob/HEAD/plugins/loom/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Research technical questions about WASM runtimes, CRDTs, mesh networking, Web3/DID, Solana, and Rust ecosystem crates for the rashk project. ([crazyverse/crazyverse](https://github.com/crazyverse/crazyverse/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md) · 2 copies)
- **researcher**: Research agent — comprehensive package, license, pattern, and reference project research. Searches npm/GitHub, checks licenses and health, finds reference implementations, identifies best practices. Consolidates: researcher + license-checker + inspiration. ([cspergel/forgedev](https://github.com/cspergel/forgedev/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Use before implementation to investigate one focused question, gather cited facts, and write a structured report without changing production files. ([ctoth/protocols-plugin](https://github.com/ctoth/protocols-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/protocols/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Deep codebase researcher with KODEGEN tools. WHEN User asks "investigate", "analyze codebase", "find all X", "research how Y works", needs multi-file investigation. WHEN NOT Simple file reads, single searches, known file locations. ([cyrup-ai/kodegen-claude-plugin](https://github.com/cyrup-ai/kodegen-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/kg/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Performs web and codebase research, cites sources, NEVER edits. ([dahui/ai-sourdough-starter](https://github.com/dahui/ai-sourdough-starter/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md) · 5 copies)
- **researcher**: Searches the web and codebase for information, gathers context, and returns concise summaries. Use for research tasks that need isolated context to avoid polluting the main conversation. ([Daimnedope/TeamAgents](https://github.com/Daimnedope/TeamAgents/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md) · 2 copies)
- **researcher**: Investiga una pregunta en la web (specs de APIs, docs de librerías, precios) y devuelve una síntesis citada. Úsalo para cualquier cosa que requiera leer muchas fuentes sin ensuciar el contexto principal. ([david-ramos-23/drava-dev-starter-kit](https://github.com/david-ramos-23/drava-dev-starter-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Owns the `research` phase — the first phase in every pipeline. Gathers evidence before the product is framed (project scope) or before the feature is designed (feature scope): prior art, existing solutions, technical feasibility, domain constraints, and competitive landscape. Produces a dated findings doc that the brainstormer (project) or architect (feature) consumes as upstream input. Does NOT make product or engineering decisions — it surfaces evidence and open questions. Outputs a dated markdown spec under the scope's research directory. ([DaylightChen/phased-dev](https://github.com/DaylightChen/phased-dev/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Deep multi source research agent. Spawn it to investigate a question thoroughly, covering technical options, market and competitor landscape, API capabilities, best practices, or "how do other people solve X", across web sources and local files. Returns a synthesized answer with cited sources, explicit confidence levels, and clear separation of fact from inference. Use for any research task meaty enough to justify a cold start, or when several research questions can run in parallel. ([ddivyanshsharmaa/fable-toolkit](https://github.com/ddivyanshsharmaa/fable-toolkit/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Read-only research agent. Use PROACTIVELY for codebase exploration, dependency analysis, documentation lookup, or any question that requires reading many files but produces no edits. ([ddobrin/plan-skills-claude](https://github.com/ddobrin/plan-skills-claude/blob/HEAD/multi-agent/orchestrator/claude-code/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Fetches and reads documentation for roles ([delta-and-beta-marchi/braze-agency](https://github.com/delta-and-beta-marchi/braze-agency/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **Researcher**: Generic research agent. Use this when no methodology-specific researcher (academic / investigative / contrarian / multi-angle / deep-investigation) is a clearly better fit. ([devergehq/locus](https://github.com/devergehq/locus/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Investiga normativa, autores, terminología, fuentes primarias y datos clave. Puebla notas.md con hallazgos verificables. NO inventa. Si algo no está confirmado, lo marca [VERIFICAR] con la pregunta concreta a resolver. ([devwspito/libroforge](https://github.com/devwspito/libroforge/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Read-only exploration / fan-out research agent (Haiku). Use to sweep the codebase, gather facts, or answer "where/what/who-calls" questions WITHOUT touching the main session's context window. Returns a tight summary, never file dumps. Never writes code. ([devx-commerce/fleet](https://github.com/devx-commerce/fleet/blob/HEAD/registry/modules/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Second agent in the content pipeline. Takes the Strategist's brief and finds 5 primary sources, 3 key statistics, and 3 contrarian data points — the differentiated material that makes a piece stand out. Use after the Strategist has approved an angle, before writing. ([DivineUchenna/flowlyy_workspace](https://github.com/DivineUchenna/flowlyy_workspace/blob/HEAD/plugins/content/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Investigates technical unknowns, compares vendors/libraries, researches security & compliance topics, and produces decision recommendations.
